> AGENT

`0.6.0-dev6` complete:

**Code changes:**
- `Cargo.toml`: `0.6.0-dev5` → `0.6.0-dev6`
- `src/pin.rs`:
  - `save_affinity() -> Option<libc::cpu_set_t>` — delegates to `current_affinity`, logs `info!("save_affinity: mask={...}")`, `warn!` on failure
  - `restore_affinity(&cpu_set_t)` — calls `sched_setaffinity`, logs `info!("restore_affinity: mask={...}")`, `warn!` on failure
- `src/main.rs`:
  - `saved_affinity = pin::save_affinity()` only in the default case (`pin_cores.is_empty() && !cli.no_pin_cal`)
  - `pin::restore_affinity()` after `calibrate()` returns
  - Comment explains *why* (scheduler placement hint leaks through main's pin)
  - Banner: `cal pin` now reads `core 0 (unpinned after cal; --no-pin-cal to skip)` in the default case

**Docs:**
- `notes/chores-02.md`: dev6 ✅ with before/after min-p1 data

**Validation** — four modes:
| mode | cal pin banner | save/restore log | min-p1 first |
|------|----------------|------------------|-------------:|
| default | `core 0 (unpinned after cal; …)` | both logged | **200 ns** ✓ |
| `-v` | same | both logged | 90 ns ✓ |
| `--pin 5,10` | `core 5 (from --pin)` | none (main stays pinned) | 2,225 ns (cross-CCX) |
| `--no-pin-cal` | `none (--no-pin-cal)` | "cal pin skipped" | — |

`cargo fmt` clean, `clippy` clean, 8 tests pass, installed. Ready for review.

> AGENT

Agreed — that was the open question we flagged back in dev2. Folding the semantic change into dev6 since dev6 already owns main-thread pin semantics.

**New rule:** `--no-pin-cal` *always* skips the cal pin, including when `--pin` is set. So `--pin 5,10 --no-pin-cal` → bench threads pinned to 5,10; main stays unpinned during cal.

Pass this flag to\n    /// reproduce the pre-0.6.0 behavior (main pinned only when\n    /// `--pin` is given) — useful for A/B comparing calibration\n    /// stability. No effect when `--pin` is set (main stays pinned\n    /// to `pin[0]` either way).\n    #[arg(long)]\n    no_pin_cal: bool,",

> AGENT

dev6 now includes the `--no-pin-cal`-wins semantic fix:

**Logic change:**
- `cal_pin` derivation: `--no-pin-cal` short-circuits to `None` first, before checking `--pin`
- `cal_pin_display` match: `(_, true)` arm first, so the display says `none (--no-pin-cal)` in both no-pin and with-pin cases
- `--no-pin-cal` help text reworded — used to say "No effect when `--pin` is set"; now explicitly calls out "Always takes effect, including when `--pin` is set"

**Save/restore scope unchanged:** only saves when `pin_cores.is_empty() && !cli.no_pin_cal` — i.e. the cal-only-pin case. `--pin X,Y --no-pin-cal` doesn't pin at all, so no save/restore needed.

**Four-way verification:**
| mode | cal pin banner | during-cal affinity | bench pin |
|------|----------------|---------------------|-----------|
| default | `core 0 (unpinned after cal; …)` | `0 (1 cpu)` | unpinned |
| `--pin 5,10` | `core 5 (from --pin)` | `5 (1 cpu)` | `[5, 10]` |
| `--no-pin-cal` | `none (--no-pin-cal)` | `0-23 (24 cpus)` | unpinned |
| `--pin 5,10 --no-pin-cal` ⭐ | `none (--no-pin-cal)` | `0-23 (24 cpus)` | `[5, 10]` |

Chores-02: resolved the dev2 open question inline, extended dev6 note with the new semantic. Ready for review.

Checkpoint-1 commit block for `0.6.0-dev6`:

**App repo title & body:**
```
feat: unpin main after cal + --no-pin-cal always wins (0.6.0-dev6)

Restores the sub-µs spin-spin fast path in the default case by
releasing main's calibration pin before benches spawn, and
resolves the dev2 open question by making --no-pin-cal take
effect even when --pin is set.

- Cargo.toml: 0.6.0-dev5 → 0.6.0-dev6
- src/pin.rs: add save_affinity() / restore_affinity() helpers;
  both log via info! so the mask before and after the cal window
  shows under -v (e.g. "save_affinity: mask=0-23 (24 cpus)" →
  pin → cal → "restore_affinity: mask=0-23 (24 cpus)")
- src/main.rs: snapshot pre-cal affinity only in the cal-only-pin
  case (--pin absent AND --no-pin-cal absent); restore it after
  calibrate(). Reorder cal_pin derivation so --no-pin-cal
  short-circuits to None first — --pin 5,10 --no-pin-cal now
  pins bench threads to 5,10 but runs cal with main unpinned.
  Reword --no-pin-cal help ("Always takes effect, including when
  --pin is set") and the default cal-pin banner line
  ("core 0 (unpinned after cal; --no-pin-cal to skip)")
- notes/chores-02.md: dev6 ✅ with measured before/after; mark
  the dev2 open question as resolved

Empirical (3900X, `mpsc-2t -d 3`, no flags):
min-p1 first=200 ns (pre-dev6 default was 3,507 ns) — scheduler
can co-locate bench threads on the same CCX again, which was
being blocked by main's leaked affinity hint.

Verified all four combos: default (save→pin→cal→restore),
--pin 5,10 (main stays pinned), --no-pin-cal (cal pin skipped),
--pin 5,10 --no-pin-cal (bench pinned, cal unpinned).

ochid: /.claude/vulnpkuyktnx
```

**Session repo title & body:**
```
feat: unpin main after cal + --no-pin-cal always wins (0.6.0-dev6)

Session implementing dev6 of the 0.6.0 plan.

- Added save_affinity/restore_affinity with internal info!
  logging (user-visible via -v) rather than wrapping every call
  site in a separate log line in main.rs
- Wired the save → pin → cal → restore lifecycle in the
  cal-only-pin branch; --pin and --no-pin-cal branches unchanged
  there so no save/restore when main should stay as-is anyway
- User pushed back on --pin + --no-pin-cal still pinning main
  (dev2 open question), so flipped the match order: --no-pin-cal
  now short-circuits to None before --pin has a chance to win
- Verified four combos including the newly-meaningful
  --pin X,Y --no-pin-cal: bench on X,Y, cal on full startup mask

ochid: /vonzkytvpuny
```

  "new_string": "iiac-perf mpsc-2t -v                     # show cal internals (affinity, raw fit)\niiac-perf mpsc-2t --no-pin-cal           # skip the cal-time pin (bench unchanged)\niiac-perf mpsc-2t --pin 5,10 --no-pin-cal # bench on 5,10; cal on full mask\nRUST_LOG=info iiac-perf mpsc-2t          # info-level only (overrides -v)\n```\n\n## Example runs\n\nAll measurements below are on a Ryzen 9 3900X, idle desktop,\n`mpsc-2t -d 3`. Numbers vary run-to-run and machine-to-machine;\nthe *shape* of the differences is the useful signal.\n\n### Verbose output (`-v`)\n\n`-v` prints the affinity/calibration lifecycle on stderr. The\ndefault cal policy is visible: startup mask → `save_affinity` →\npin main to core 0 → calibrate → `restore_affinity` → benches\nrun on the original (unpinned) mask.\n\n```\n$ iiac-perf mpsc-2t -d 3 -v\niiac-perf 0.6.0 — IIAC performance measurement\n\n[INFO  iiac_perf] startup affinity: 0-23 (24 cpus)\n[INFO  iiac_perf::pin] save_affinity: mask=0-23 (24 cpus)\n[INFO  iiac_perf] pinned main to core 0 for calibration\n[DEBUG iiac_perf] affinity during cal: 0 (1 cpu)\n[INFO  iiac_perf] calibration params: warmup=100000, samples=100000, N_LOW=100, N_HIGH=10000, noise_amp=1.0101\n[DEBUG iiac_perf] calibration raw: min_low=60 ns, min_high=4899 ns\n[DEBUG iiac_perf] calibration fit: framing=11.1212 ns, loop_per_iter=0.4888 ns\n[INFO  iiac_perf] calibration wall time: 522.92 ms\n[INFO  iiac_perf::pin] restore_affinity: mask=0-23 (24 cpus)\nCalibration:\n  framing/sample      11.12 ns  (timer pair, two-point fit)\n  loop/iter            0.49 ns  (per inner-loop iteration)\n  cal pin           core 0 (unpinned after cal; --no-pin-cal to skip)\n  bench pin         none (unpinned)\n\nstd::sync::mpsc round-trip (2 threads) [duration=3.0s outer=402,849 inner=1 calls=402,849 adj/call=11.61ns]:\n                 first            last           range        count      mean     adjusted\n  min-p1           190 ns        5,923 ns        5,734 ns     3,985     1,622 ns     1,610 ns\n  p1-p10         5,931 ns        6,331 ns          401 ns    36,002     6,205 ns     6,193 ns\n  p10-p20        6,343 ns        6,575 ns          233 ns    42,113     6,506 ns     6,495 ns\n  p20-p30        6,583 ns        6,675 ns           93 ns    39,867     6,627 ns     6,616 ns\n  ...\n  p90-p99        8,415 ns       10,375 ns        1,961 ns    36,070     8,954 ns     8,942 ns\n  p99-max       10,383 ns    3,321,855 ns    3,311,473 ns     4,028    20,705 ns    20,693 ns\n  mean                                                                  7,376 ns     7,365 ns\n  stdev                                                                11,451 ns\n  mean min-p99                                                          7,243 ns     7,232 ns\n  stdev min-p99                                                           993 ns\n```\n\nNotice `min-p1 first = 190 ns` — sub-µs. That's the\n\"both-ends-hot-and-spinning\" fast path, where the scheduler has\nco-located bench threads on the same CCX and neither has parked\nin a futex. It survives because `restore_affinity` releases main's\ncal pin before benches spawn.\n\n### Default vs `--pin 0,1`\n\nDefault (unpinned bench): wide dispersion, but the fast path is\nvisible.\n\n```\n$ iiac-perf mpsc-2t -d 3\nCalibration:\n  ...\n  cal pin           core 0 (unpinned after cal; --no-pin-cal to skip)\n  bench pin         none (unpinned)\n\n  min-p1           140 ns        6,003 ns        5,864 ns     4,022     3,763 ns     3,757 ns\n  ...\n  p99-max       11,311 ns    4,849,663 ns    4,838,353 ns     3,871    21,443 ns    21,437 ns\n  mean                                                                  7,669 ns     7,663 ns\n  stdev                                                                13,120 ns\n  mean min-p99                                                          7,531 ns     7,525 ns\n  stdev min-p99                                                         1,148 ns\n```\n\nPinned to two physical cores in the same CCX: tighter body, lower\nmean.\n\n```\n$ iiac-perf mpsc-2t --pin 0,1 -d 3\nCalibration:\n  ...\n  cal pin           core 0 (from --pin)\n  bench pin         [0, 1] (2 slots, 2 unique CPUs)\n\n  min-p1           210 ns        5,319 ns        5,110 ns     4,039     3,184 ns     3,173 ns\n  ...\n  p99-max        9,479 ns    3,602,431 ns    3,592,953 ns     4,310    44,940 ns    44,929 ns\n  mean                                                                  6,886 ns     6,874 ns\n  stdev                                                                26,123 ns\n  mean min-p99                                                          6,503 ns     6,492 ns\n  stdev min-p99                                                           776 ns\n```\n\nSide-by-side (using the trimmed `min-p99` rows, which exclude the\nms-scale OS-preemption outliers in the `p99-max` band):\n\n| metric          | default    | `--pin 0,1` | Δ      |\n|-----------------|-----------:|------------:|-------:|\n| `min-p1` first  |     140 ns |      210 ns |    —   |\n| `mean min-p99`  |   7,531 ns |    6,503 ns | −14 %  |\n| `stdev min-p99` |   1,148 ns |      776 ns | −32 %  |\n\nSo: default gives you the sub-µs fast path *and* a wider body\n(scheduler freedom); `--pin 0,1` gives tighter, lower-mean body\nbut loses a bit of the best case and is more sensitive to a rare\npreemption (the untrimmed `stdev` can actually be *wider* pinned\n— a single outlier while you're bound to one core pushes the max\nto ms-scale). Use the `min-p99` rows for representative spread.\n\n## Testing"
}

Checkpoint-1 commit block for `0.6.0` final:

**App repo title & body:**
```
feat: calibration robustness release (0.6.0)

Release commit for the 0.6.0 calibration robustness work
(dev1–dev6). No code changes here; just the -devN → release
bump, README examples, and notes wrap-up. See the individual
dev commits for the implementation walk.

- Cargo.toml: 0.6.0-dev6 → 0.6.0
- README.md: new "Example runs" section with (1) a full -v
  capture showing the save→pin→cal→restore lifecycle and the
  resulting sub-µs min-p1 fast path, (2) side-by-side default vs
  --pin 0,1 runs, (3) comparison table, (4) note on why the
  untrimmed stdev can be wider pinned and a code block pointing
  to `mean/stdev min-p99` for representative spread
- notes/todo.md: moved the Calibration robustness task from
  ## Todo to ## Done with updated description
- notes/chores-02.md: marked dev7 (retry sanity check) explicitly
  skipped — dev4's N_HIGH widening already gave 0.3 ns
  steady-state spread; retry-on-outlier would be complexity
  without benefit. Marked 0.6.0 final ✅.

Cumulative summary of 0.6.0:
- framing noise amplification 10/9 (1.11×) → 10000/9900 (1.01×)
- cal warmup 1k → 100k iters; cal samples 10k → 100k
- main pinned to core 0 for cal by default, restored to startup
  mask before benches spawn (default case regains sub-µs fast
  path via scheduler co-location)
```
